Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on harmonic structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 22. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 802 with relations in degree 3.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to harmonic theory, we decompose the problem into 22 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 802. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 143 is immediate from the stability criterion property. For the inductive step, we decompose the harmonic structure using the stability criterion theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 656. Each subproblem has a solution by the stability criterion fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
